- **loess-batch-correction-of-lc-ms-feature-tables**: Use when a QC-filtered LC-MS feature table contains pooled/intrastudy QC injections in run order and time-related signal drift must be removed from study-sample intensities before normalization or statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/loess-batch-correction-of-lc-ms-feature-tables))
- **loess-coverage-smoothing-genomic-tracks**: Use when differentially expressed uTAR coverage tracks need smoothing and peak-coordinate extraction (FWHM) to define the nucleotide sequence that will be BLASTn-annotated. - **loess-drift-modeling**: Use when pooled/intrastudy QC samples in an LC–MS batch (e.g., mzML files from MetaboLights MTBLS1919) show time-related systematic variance — signal drift along PC1 of a PCA score plot — that must be modeled and removed over injection order. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/loess-drift-modeling))
- **loess-drift-trend-fitting**: Use when MRM sample files contain QC samples and systematic signal drift across injection order must be corrected per compound, e.g. BQC regression slope != 0 before correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/loess-drift-trend-fitting))
- **loess-fit-diagnostic-visualization**: Use when running HashSeq's inferTrueSequences on 16S rRNA gene FASTQ data and you need to verify the LOESS regression is a good fit above the abundance threshold before trusting variant calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/loess-fit-diagnostic-visualization))
- **loess-methylation-profile-smoothing**: Use when comparing per-site methylation frequencies from nanopore tools (augmented BED per-site tables) against WGBS along targeted regions, to reveal local CpG island hypomethylation patterns and tool-specific local discrepancies. - **loess-regression-fitting**: Use when you need to model the mean and standard deviation of one-mismatch child abundances as a smooth function of parent abundance, or to empirically choose the abundance threshold above which a LOESS background error model is a good fit. - **loess-regression-residual-computation**: Use when computing per-gene entropy-reduction statistics (ds) from a gene-by-cell scRNA-seq count matrix to feed the ROGUE cluster-purity calculation, or when modeling the expected mean-expression vs. sample-entropy relationship. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/loess-regression-residual-computation))
- **loess-smoothing-of-pvalue-curves**: Use when moving-window age-comparison tests (e.g., MANOVA on windowed profiles) yield noisy per-age p-value curves that must be smoothed across several bandwidths and combined before calling breakpoint ages. - **loess-spline-eic-smoothing**: Use when deconvolving MS1-only LC/HRMS data with IDSL.CSA: you need Pearson correlation coefficients between co-eluting peaks' EICs to be robust to noise and irregular sampling before grouping ions into CSA composite spectra clusters.
